# Planner -- Implementation Plan

> **Module**: Planner
> **Layer**: L3 (K1 Cognitive Architecture)
> **Upstream**: Orchestrator (L2) sends PlanRequest via IMailboxPort
> **Downstream**: Capability Fabric (L2.5) for discovery, Model Hub for LLM, Bridge (K0) for WAL persist
> **Pipeline**: SKETCH -> EXPAND -> VALIDATE -> COMMIT (4-stage, ~12s P50)
> **Invariants**: 12 hard invariants (PLAN-01 through PLAN-12)
> **Ports**: 7 hexagonal ports (IMailboxPort, ILLMPort, IFabricRetrievalPort, IStateReadPort, IBridgePort, IDeltaEmitPort, IEventPort)
> **Services**: 7 internal services (~335 estimated tests)
> **Adapters**: 14 total (7 production + 7 test)
> **Files**: 39 required per wiring.contract.yaml

---

## What the Planner IS

- The ONLY LLM-powered planning component in K1
- A 4-stage pipeline that transforms PlanRequest into CommittedPlan
- A hexagonal module with strict port boundaries
- A read-only consumer of SessionState (PLAN-01: never writes)
- A plan producer that never executes capabilities (PLAN-06)

## What the Planner IS NOT

- Not an executor (Orchestrator owns DAG execution)
- Not a state writer (Concierge owns SessionState writes)
- Not a capability runner (Fabric owns capability execution)
- Not an LLM gateway (Model Hub owns LLM connectivity)
- Not a conversation manager (Concierge owns user interaction)

---

## Services

| Service | Responsibility | Estimated Tests |
|---------|---------------|-----------------|
| PipelineController | Stage orchestration, budget injection, cancel protocol | ~80 |
| SketchService | Stage 1: rough plan via LLM (~2K tokens) | ~60 |
| ExpandService | Stage 2: tool mapping + parameterization (~1K tokens) | ~50 |
| ValidateService | Stage 3: deterministic checks + LLM arbiter (~500 tokens) | ~45 |
| ToolCallRouter | Routes 4 discovery tools, enforces PLAN-05 (max 6 calls) | ~35 |
| HILCoordinator | Clarification + approval flows, max 2 rounds (PLAN-10) | ~35 |
| CommitService | Stage 4: deterministic assembly, zero LLM (PLAN-03) | ~30 |

## Ports

| Port | Direction | Purpose |
|------|-----------|---------|
| IMailboxPort | Inbound | Receives PlanRequest from Orchestrator |
| ILLMPort | Outbound | LLM calls for SKETCH, EXPAND, VALIDATE |
| IFabricRetrievalPort | Outbound | Capability discovery, prompt retrieval |
| IStateReadPort | Read-only | SessionState context reads |
| IBridgePort | Outbound | WAL persist, K0 audit |
| IDeltaEmitPort | Outbound | Progress deltas to Delta Bus |
| IEventPort | Pub/Sub | Plan events, HIL coordination |

## Invariants

| ID | Rule |
|----|------|
| PLAN-01 | Never writes SessionState |
| PLAN-02 | Read-only tools only |
| PLAN-03 | COMMIT is deterministic (zero LLM) |
| PLAN-04 | 45s max pipeline timeout |
| PLAN-05 | Max 6 tool calls per plan |
| PLAN-06 | Never executes capabilities |
| PLAN-07 | Token budget deferred V2 |
| PLAN-08 | All capabilities must exist in Fabric |
| PLAN-09 | Plan must be DAG (no cycles) |
| PLAN-10 | HIL max 2 rounds |
| PLAN-11 | All LLM calls carry budget |
| PLAN-12 | Micro-replan adjusts remaining steps only |

## Dependency Map

| Dependency | Layer | Interaction | Test Adapter |
|------------|-------|-------------|--------------|
| Orchestrator | K1 L2 | Sends PlanRequest, receives CommittedPlan | MockMailboxAdapter |
| Model Hub | K1 | LLM inference calls | MockLLMAdapter |
| Capability Fabric | K1 L2.5 | Discovery tools, capability registry | MockFabricRetrievalAdapter |
| SessionState | K1 L5 | Context snapshots (read-only) | MockStateReadAdapter |
| Bridge (K0) | K1 L6 | WAL persist, audit writes | MockBridgeAdapter |
| Delta Bus | K1 Coordination | Progress deltas | MockDeltaEmitAdapter |
| K1 Event Bus | K1 Coordination | Plan events, HIL events | MockEventAdapter |

### Dependency Layer Rules (SS30.6)

> **CRITICAL**: No file in Layer N imports from Layer N+1 or higher. Violations cause circular imports and are rejected.

```text
Layer 0: types.py, events.py, config.py, plan_fsm.py     -> imports: stdlib, k1.orchestrator.types, k1.fabric.types, k1.model_hub.types
Layer 1: ports/*                                          -> imports: Layer 0 only
Layer 2: adapters/*                                       -> imports: Layer 0, Layer 1
Layer 3: services/*                                       -> imports: Layer 0, Layer 1
Layer 4: stages/*                                         -> imports: Layer 0, Layer 1, Layer 3
Layer 5: pipeline_controller.py                           -> imports: Layer 0, Layer 1, Layer 4
Layer 6: planner_agent.py                                 -> imports: Layer 0, Layer 1, Layer 5
Layer 7: factory.py                                       -> imports: ALL layers (only file permitted to cross all layers)
Layer 8: __init__.py                                      -> imports: re-exports only
```

---

## Per-Issue Acceptance Criteria

> **Purpose**: Every issue in this plan has an explicit DONE-WHEN checklist by issue category. AI coding agents MUST verify all criteria before marking an issue complete. No issue is complete until ALL applicable criteria pass.

### Category A: ADR Issues (M1 Epic 1.1)

```
DONE WHEN:
- [ ] Markdown file exists at docs/architecture/decisions-K0/PLAN-{NNN}-{slug}.md
- [ ] ADR follows template: Title, Status, Context, Decision, Consequences, Alternatives Rejected
- [ ] Decision section references specific planner.md SS# sections
- [ ] No Python code created (ADRs are documents only)
- [ ] File is valid Markdown (no broken links, no syntax errors)
```

### Category B: Type / Dataclass Issues (M1 Epic 1.2)

```
DONE WHEN:
- [ ] File exists at specified path (k1/planner/types.py, config.py, plan_fsm.py, or events.py)
- [ ] Class is @dataclass(frozen=True) with all fields typed
- [ ] All field types use exact types from Import Path Map above (no approximations)
- [ ] __all__ in file includes the new symbol
- [ ] No I/O, no port references, no service logic in dataclass (pure data)
- [ ] Imports follow Layer 0 rules: stdlib + k1.orchestrator.types + k1.fabric.types + k1.model_hub.types ONLY
- [ ] If shared type (PlanRequest, CommittedPlan, etc.): verify existing definition, add re-export in k1/planner/__init__.py
- [ ] mypy --strict k1/planner/types.py passes (or target file)
- [ ] ruff check k1/planner/types.py passes (or target file)
- [ ] Corresponding pytest test exists with at least: construction test, frozen immutability test, field validation test
```

### Category C: Contract Issues (M1 Epic 1.3)

```
DONE WHEN:
- [ ] YAML file exists at k1/contracts/modules/planner/{name}.contract.yaml
- [ ] YAML is valid (parseable by PyYAML with no errors)
- [ ] required_files count matches 39 (for wiring contract) per SS30.2
- [ ] All event topic strings match k1/planner/events.py constants exactly
- [ ] All port names match k1/planner/ports/ Protocol class names exactly
- [ ] Contract passes schema validation if a JSON Schema exists for contract format
```

### Category D: Port Protocol Issues (M1 Epic 1.4)

```
DONE WHEN:
- [ ] File exists at k1/planner/ports/{port_name}.py matching [F##] spec
- [ ] Class is @runtime_checkable typing.Protocol with all methods declared
- [ ] All method signatures match planner.md SS15 exactly (params, return types, async/sync)
- [ ] Method bodies are ... (Ellipsis) ONLY -- no implementation logic
- [ ] PLAN-01 check (IStateReadPort): NO write/update/set/delete methods exist
- [ ] PLAN-06 check (IFabricRetrievalPort): NO invoke_capability/execute methods exist
- [ ] Imports use ONLY Layer 0 types + external types per Import Path Map
- [ ] Port re-exported in k1/planner/ports/__init__.py [F11]
- [ ] mypy --strict passes on port file
- [ ] ruff check passes on port file
```

### Category E: Event Schema & Config Issues (M1 Epic 1.5)

```
DONE WHEN:
- [ ] Topic constant string matches planner.md SS28 exactly (e.g., "k1.planner.plan.ready.v1")
- [ ] Payload dataclass is @dataclass(frozen=True) with all fields typed
- [ ] Topic constant and payload class are defined in k1/planner/events.py [F06]
- [ ] For PlannerConfig: all fields have correct defaults per SS30.5.1 F07
- [ ] For PlannerConfig: __post_init__ validates all constraints (timeouts > 0, temperatures in range, etc.)
- [ ] For PlannerConfig: from_dict() class method handles overrides and rejects unknown keys
- [ ] mypy --strict passes
- [ ] ruff check passes
```

### Category F: FSM Issues (M2 Epic 2.1)

```
DONE WHEN:
- [ ] File exists at k1/planner/plan_fsm.py [F04]
- [ ] PlanState enum has exactly 10 members matching SS18.1
- [ ] TRANSITION_TABLE has exactly 25 legal transitions matching SS18.4
- [ ] PlanStateMachine.transition() raises IllegalStateTransitionError for any transition NOT in table
- [ ] PlanStateMachine.force_failed() works from all active states, raises from terminal/IDLE
- [ ] PlanStateMachine.reset() works from terminal states only
- [ ] on_transition callback is called AFTER state update (observer sees new state)
- [ ] Imports are Layer 0 ONLY (stdlib + types)
- [ ] Unit tests exist: legal transitions, illegal transitions, terminal reset, force_failed, callback invocation
- [ ] mypy --strict passes
- [ ] ruff check passes
```

### Category G: Service / Stage / Controller Issues (M2-M5)

```
DONE WHEN:

- [ ] Every chaos scenario maps to a named recovery path in SS19

- [ ] No resource leak: plan_lock released, counters reset, mailbox accepting
- [ ] For documentation: content traceable to planner.md section numbers (no speculation)

```

### V1 / V2 Scope Guard

> **DO NOT IMPLEMENT** (V2 deferred). Any issue that accidentally builds these features must be rejected.

| V2 Feature | Guard |
|------------|-------|
| LLMGatewayAdapter production use | V1 uses TestLLMAdapter in ALL environments (SS16.1.2) |
| PLAN-07 token_budget per PlanStep | Field removed from PlanStep in V1 |
| Full crash recovery (WAL resume) | V1 always discards partial state on restart (SS23.6) |
| Multi-plan concurrency | V1 uses single asyncio.Lock, max 1 concurrent plan (SS24.1) |
| Delta rate limiting | V1 inherently bounded at ~1.3 deltas/s (SS22.4, 8.4.3) |
| Weighted Fair Queuing in mailbox | V1 uses single INTERACTIVE priority, FIFO only (SS4.1) |
| Preemptive micro-replan | V1 micro-replan awaits _plan_lock (SS24.3) |

## Milestone Overview

| Milestone | Name | Epics | Focus |
|-----------|------|-------|-------|
| M1 | Foundation | 5 | ADRs, core types, contracts, port definitions, event schemas & config |
| M2 | Pipeline Infrastructure | 3 | Plan FSM, PipelineController, PlannerAgent |
| M3 | Stage Services | 4 | SketchService, ExpandService, ValidateService, CommitService |
| M4 | Support Services | 2 | ToolCallRouter, HILCoordinator |
| M5 | Micro-Replan | 1 | Abbreviated pipeline, discovery overlap, remaining-steps adjustment |
| M6 | Adapters & Factory | 4 | 14 adapters (7 prod + 7 test), PlannerFactory, kernel wiring |
| M7 | Testing | 8 | Unit (~335), integration, contract, invariant, lifecycle, performance |
| M8 | Observability | 4 | Metrics, tracing, structured logging, delta emissions |
| M9 | Production Readiness | 4 | Performance benchmarks, chaos testing, documentation, final validation |

---

## Milestone 1: Foundation

> **Goal**: Establish architectural decisions, define core types, create contracts, port definitions, and event schemas.

### Epic 1.1: ADR Review & Creation

**Goal**: Review existing ADRs that govern Planner behavior, and create new ADRs for Planner-specific architectural decisions. All ADRs are Markdown documents -- no Python code in this epic.

> **Reference**: planner.md §2 (Invariant Table), §9 (COMMIT), §10 (Micro-Replan), §11 (Discovery Tools), §12 (HIL), §13 (LLM Budget), §20 (Circuit Breakers), §24 (Concurrency Model).
>
> - ADR-PLAN-001 (Concurrency Model) **decides** V1 single-plan `asyncio.Lock` concurrency. Gates: `PlannerAgent._plan_lock` (F02), `MailboxAdapter.queue` depth-5 (F28), Plan FSM IDLE check (F04).
> - ADR-PLAN-002 (LLM Budget) **decides** per-stage token budget and timeout injection. Gates: `PipelineController.budget_injection()` (F03), `PlannerConfig` budget fields (F07), PLAN-11 enforcement in all `HubRequest.constraints`.
> - ADR-PLAN-003 (Micro-Replan) **decides** abbreviated 3-stage pipeline, 10s timeout, max 1 per DAG. Gates: `PipelineController.micro_replan()` (F03), MICRO_SKETCH/MICRO_EXPAND/MICRO_VALIDATE FSM states (F04), overlap heuristic in pipeline.
> - ADR-PLAN-004 (HIL Protocol) **decides** clarification + approval flows, 2-round budget, timeout handling. Gates: `HILCoordinator` (F26), 4 HIL event schemas (F06), PLAN-10 round counter.
> - ADR-PLAN-005 (CommitService Determinism) **decides** zero-LLM COMMIT stage, deterministic assembly. Gates: `CommitService` constructor has no `ILLMPort` param (F23), PLAN-03 enforced at interface level.
> - ADR-PLAN-006 (Discovery Tool Routing) **decides** 4 read-only tools, 3-port routing, PLAN-05 max 6 calls. Gates: `ToolCallRouter` routing table (F25), PLAN-02/PLAN-05/PLAN-06 enforcement.
> - **File location**: `k1/docs/adrs/PLAN-001-*.md` through `PLAN-008-*.md`. ADR naming: `PLAN-{number:03d}-{slug}.md`.
> - **Anti-hallucination**: ADRs are NOT code deliverables. Do NOT create Python files for this epic. Do NOT implement any runtime behavior. ADRs are Markdown documents that record decisions.

| Issue | Title | Status | Deliverable | Details |
|-------|-------|--------|-------------|---------|
| 1.1.1 | Review ADR-0007 (4-Stage Planning Pipeline) | DONE | k1/docs/adrs/PLAN-007-pipeline-review-adr-0007.md | **Review scope**: Confirm ADR-0007 still accurately describes the 4-stage pipeline (SKETCH -> EXPAND -> VALIDATE -> COMMIT). **Verify alignment**: planner.md §5 defines 26-step pipeline flow; confirm ADR covers stage responsibilities (SKETCH=rough plan ~2K tokens, EXPAND=tool mapping ~1K tokens, VALIDATE=deterministic checks + LLM arbiter ~500 tokens, COMMIT=deterministic assembly 0 tokens). **Check**: per-stage timing budgets (p50: 4s + 3s + 2s + 0.1s = ~12s total per §21), token allocation (SKETCH=2048, EXPAND=1024, VALIDATE=512, total ~3.5K per §13.3). **Action**: annotate review findings, flag any drift between ADR-0007 and planner.md §5. No Python code. |
| 1.1.2 | Review ADR-0017 (Single Writer) for Planner compliance | DONE | k1/docs/adrs/PLAN-008-single-writer-compliance-adr-0017.md | **Review scope**: Confirm Planner complies with ADR-0017 Single Writer principle. **PLAN-01 compliance**: Planner has `IStateReadPort` (read-only, no write methods per §15.5) and NO `IStateWritePort`. Enforced at interface level -- port Protocol has no `write_*` methods. **PLAN-02 compliance**: All 4 discovery tools are read-only (§11.5.1 routing table: discover_capabilities, find_relevant_prompts, query_planning_context, recall_for_planning -- zero write/execute routes). **PLAN-06 compliance**: `IFabricRetrievalPort` has discovery methods only, no `invoke_capability` (§15.4). **Action**: document compliance matrix mapping ADR-0017 requirements to Planner port/invariant enforcement points. No Python code. |
| 1.1.3 | Create ADR: Planner Concurrency Model (V1 single plan) | DONE | k1/docs/adrs/PLAN-001-concurrency-model.md | **Decision**: V1 uses single-plan concurrency via `asyncio.Lock` (§24.1). **Context**: PlannerAgent runs as single-threaded async actor in one Python event loop. No thread pool, no multiprocessing, no parallel plan execution. **Mechanism**: `_plan_lock: asyncio.Lock` acquired at PLAN_START step 3, released at PLAN_END step 2. FIFO for waiters (§24.1.1). **Mailbox**: depth-5 FIFO queue, INTERACTIVE priority only, rejects with PlanAck(REJECTED) when full (§4.1). **Micro-replan**: bypasses mailbox queue but `await`s `_plan_lock.acquire()` -- blocks if plan in flight, 10s caller-side timeout means it almost always times out during active plan (§24.3). **Cancel**: cooperative flag-based, checked between stages (§18.5.3). **V2 direction**: possible preemption of SKETCH stage (§24.4, not yet designed). **Alternatives rejected**: (1) thread pool -- adds complexity, GIL limits benefit; (2) concurrent plans -- resource contention on LLM calls, V2 deferred. |
| 1.1.4 | Create ADR: LLM Budget Enforcement Strategy | DONE | k1/docs/adrs/PLAN-002-llm-budget-enforcement.md | **Decision**: Every LLM call carries explicit budget in `HubRequest.constraints` (PLAN-11 per §13.3). **Budget table**: SKETCH={max_tokens:2048, timeout_ms:8000, temperature:0.7}, EXPAND={max_tokens:1024, timeout_ms:5000, temperature:0.3}, VALIDATE={max_tokens:512, timeout_ms:3000, temperature:0.2}, COMMIT=no LLM (PLAN-03). Total ~3.5K tokens. **Injection point**: `PipelineController` stamps next stage's `HubRequest.constraints` at STAGE_TRANSITION (§23.3 step 6). **Enforcement boundary**: Model Hub enforces `max_tokens` and `timeout_ms` -- Planner trusts Model Hub for enforcement, Planner stamps for declaration (§13.3.2). **Token tracking**: `stage_token_usage[stage] += HubResponse.metadata.usage.total_tokens` after each LLM call (§13.3.3). Reported in PLAN_END delta. **5 LLM call types**: LLM-1 (SKETCH main), LLM-2 (EXPAND main), LLM-3 (VALIDATE arbiter), LLM-4a (HIL clarification), LLM-4b (HIL approval draft). Plus micro-replan variants LLM-M1/M2/M3 (§13.1). **PLAN-07**: V1 deferred -- per-step `token_budget` on PlanStep removed from types. |
| 1.1.5 | Create ADR: Micro-Replan Scope & Limits | DONE | k1/docs/adrs/PLAN-003-micro-replan-scope.md | **Decision**: Micro-replan runs abbreviated 3-stage pipeline (MICRO_SKETCH -> MICRO_EXPAND -> MICRO_VALIDATE -> COMMIT) with 10s total budget (§10). **Trigger**: Orchestrator's `MicroReplanCheckpoint` guard after wave completion when discoveries or failures affect remaining steps (ORCH-13). **Protocol**: `IPlannerMailbox.micro_replan(MicroReplanRequest) -> CommittedPlan` -- synchronous, bypasses mailbox queue (§4.2). **PLAN-12**: adjusts remaining steps only, completed steps frozen. **Scope restrictions**: no HIL interaction, max 1 per DAG execution, ToolCallRouter counter resets to 0 (§10.3). **Timing budget**: Micro-SKETCH timeout_ms=5000, Micro-EXPAND timeout_ms=3000, Micro-VALIDATE timeout_ms=2000 (§10.3.6). **Overlap heuristic**: 3-way field match (exact, substring forward, substring reverse), O(D*S*P), <1ms (§10.2.2). **Concurrency**: blocks on `_plan_lock` -- if plan in flight, caller times out at 10s (§24.3). |
| 1.1.6 | Create ADR: HIL Coordination Protocol | DONE | k1/docs/adrs/PLAN-004-hil-coordination-protocol.md | **Decision**: 2 HIL interaction points managed by HILCoordinator (§12). **PLAN-10**: max 2 rounds total per plan, then best-effort. **Clarification** (Stage 1 SKETCH, §12.2): triggered when SketchService detects ambiguity in intent, multi-interpretation capability matches, or missing required context. Flow: Planner emits `k1.hil.clarification.v1{request_id, question, context}` -> Concierge surfaces to user -> user responds -> `k1.hil.clarification_response.v1` -> HILCoordinator resolves. Timeout: 60s default, proceed with best-effort on timeout. **Approval** (Stage 3 VALIDATE, §12.3): triggered when plan has high-risk side effects, elevated safety band operations, or estimated cost above threshold. Flow: Planner emits `k1.hil.approval_request.v1{request_id, summary, options, side_effects, safety_assessment}` -> user approves/rejects/modifies. Timeout: 120s default, PLAN FAILED on timeout. **Port wiring**: HILCoordinator uses ILLMPort (generate HIL messages) + IEventPort (publish/subscribe HIL events). **Namespace split**: Planning-time HIL events (k1.hil.clarification/approval) are distinct from execution-time HIL events (k1.hil.override/fallback) owned by Orchestrator (§12.1). |
| 1.1.7 | Create ADR: CommitService Determinism Guarantee (PLAN-03) | DONE | k1/docs/adrs/PLAN-005-commit-determinism.md | **Decision**: Stage 4 COMMIT is fully deterministic with zero LLM calls (PLAN-03 per §9). **Enforcement**: `CommitService` constructor takes `(bridge_port: IBridgePort, delta_port: IDeltaEmitPort, event_port: IEventPort)` -- NO `ILLMPort` parameter. Enforced at constructor level, not just convention (§30.5.3 F23). **Assembly steps** (§9.2): (1) Generate `plan_id` (UUID), (2) collect PlanStep list from ExpandedPlan, (3) build dependency dict, (4) assign `created_at` timestamp, (5) construct `CommittedPlan` frozen dataclass. **WAL persistence** (§9.3): `IBridgePort.persist_plan(CommittedPlan)` -- fire-and-forget, plan validity does NOT depend on WAL success. Idempotent upsert via `plan_id` dedup key. **Event emission**: publish `k1.planner.plan.ready.v1{CommittedPlan}` via IEventPort. **Delta**: emit `k1.planner.delta.v1{type: "plan_committed", plan_id}`. **Token usage**: exactly 0 tokens consumed in COMMIT stage. **Rationale**: deterministic COMMIT ensures reproducible plan output given same ExpandedPlan+ValidationVerdict input. Simplifies testing, debugging, and crash recovery. |
| 1.1.8 | Create ADR: Discovery Tool Selection & Routing | DONE | k1/docs/adrs/PLAN-006-discovery-tool-routing.md | **Decision**: 4 read-only discovery tools routed through ToolCallRouter to 3 backend ports (§11). **Routing table** (§11.5.1): `discover_capabilities` -> IFabricRetrievalPort, `find_relevant_prompts` -> IFabricRetrievalPort, `query_planning_context` -> IStateReadPort, `recall_for_planning` -> IBridgePort. **PLAN-05**: max 6 tool calls per plan -- ToolCallRouter maintains atomic counter, rejects with BudgetExhaustedError at limit (§11.5.2). **Budget allocation**: SKETCH uses 3 (discover+context+recall), EXPAND uses 2-3 (discover refined+prompts+optional), VALIDATE/COMMIT use 0. **PLAN-02**: all tools are read-only, zero side effects. No action/execute/write tools in routing table. **PLAN-06**: IFabricRetrievalPort has discovery methods only, no `invoke_capability`. **Retry policy** (§11.5.3): discover_capabilities 50ms/1 retry, find_relevant_prompts 50ms/1 retry, query_planning_context 10ms/0 retries, recall_for_planning 100ms/0 retries. Retries do NOT double-count against budget. **Concurrency**: SKETCH dispatches 3 calls via `asyncio.gather`; EXPAND dispatches 2-3 concurrently (§11.5.4). **Fallback**: on all-tools-fail, LLM generates plan from PlanRequest.intent alone (valid but minimal). |

### Epic 1.2: Core Types & Dataclasses

**Goal**: Create all Planner domain types as Python dataclasses. These are the internal data structures used across pipeline stages and the shared types imported from Orchestrator.

> **Reference**: planner.md §4.5 (Message Envelopes), §6.5 (SketchResult), §7.4 (ExpandedPlan), §8.3.3 (ValidationVerdict), §18 (Plan FSM States), §22.4.1 (DeltaPayload), §30.5.1 F05/F06/F07 (File Specs), §32 (Type Ownership Reference).
>
> - **Shared types** (PlanRequest, CommittedPlan, PlanStep, PlanAck, MicroReplanRequest, ConditionExpr, Discovery, FailureContext) are defined in `k1/orchestrator/types.py` -- Planner imports them but MUST NOT redefine them (§32).
> - **Planner-internal types** (SketchResult, ExpandedPlan, ValidationVerdict, StageContext, DeltaPayload, RoughStep, ValidationIssue) are defined in `k1/planner/types.py` [F05] -- they flow between stages inside the pipeline but never cross the Planner boundary.
> - **External type imports**: `ScoredCapability` from `k1/fabric/types.py`, `PlanStep` from `k1/orchestrator/types.py` (used inside ExpandedPlan), `HubRequest`/`HubResponse` from `k1/model_hub/types.py`, `SessionSnapshot` from `k1/fabric/ports/state_reader.py`, `RetrievalResult` from `k1/fabric/types.py`.
> - **Import graph**: `k1.planner.types` imports from `k1.orchestrator.types` (PlanStep, PlanRequest) and `k1.fabric.types` (ScoredCapability) but NEVER from any service or port module (no circular deps per §30.6 Layer 0).

| Issue | Title | Status | Deliverable | Details |
|-------|-------|--------|-------------|---------|
| 1.2.1 | PlanRequest dataclass | DONE | k1/orchestrator/types.py (shared, already exists) | **Ownership**: Defined in `k1/orchestrator/types.py` per §32. Planner re-exports from `k1/planner/__init__.py`. **Signature** (§4.5): `@dataclass(frozen=True) PlanRequest(request_id: str, intent: str, constraints: Dict[str, Any], context: SessionSnapshot, trace_id: str, timeout_ms: int)`. **Defaults**: request_id=uuid4(), constraints={}, timeout_ms=45000 (CB_PLANNER). **Validation**: intent non-empty; trace_id non-empty. **Planner receives via**: `IPlannerMailbox.enqueue()` (async, fire-and-forget) or as part of pipeline context. **Key contract**: Planner echoes `request_id` back in `CommittedPlan.request_id` for PendingPlanContext correlation. **Action for this issue**: verify existing definition in orchestrator types matches planner.md §4.5 spec. Add re-export in `k1/planner/__init__.py` [F01]. |
| 1.2.2 | CommittedPlan dataclass | DONE | k1/orchestrator/types.py (shared, already exists) | **Ownership**: Defined in `k1/orchestrator/types.py` per §32. Planner re-exports from `k1/planner/__init__.py`. **Signature** (§4.5): `@dataclass(frozen=True) CommittedPlan(plan_id: str, request_id: str, intent: str, steps: List[PlanStep], trace_id: str, dependencies: Dict[str, List[str]], estimated_duration_ms: int, created_at: float)`. **Validation**: plan_id non-empty (UUID assigned by CommitService); request_id non-empty (echoed from PlanRequest); steps non-empty; dependencies keys subset of step IDs; no cycles in dependency graph. **Built by**: CommitService (§9.2) from ExpandedPlan + ValidationVerdict. **Emitted via**: `IEventPort.publish("k1.planner.plan.ready.v1", committed_plan)`. **WAL**: persisted via `IBridgePort.persist_plan()` fire-and-forget. **Action**: verify existing orchestrator type matches, add re-export in `k1/planner/__init__.py`. |
| 1.2.3 | SketchResult dataclass | DONE | k1/planner/types.py [F05] | **Internal type** -- does NOT cross Planner boundary (§6.5). Flows: SketchService -> PipelineController -> ExpandService. `@dataclass(frozen=True)`. **Signature**: `SketchResult(rough_steps: List[RoughStep], capability_candidates: List[ScoredCapability], rationale: str)`. **RoughStep** (nested): `@dataclass(frozen=True) RoughStep(intent: str, suggested_capability: Optional[str], depends_on: List[str], confidence: float)`. **Validation** (§6.5): (1) `rough_steps` must be non-empty list, (2) each step must have non-empty `intent`, (3) `rationale` must be non-empty, (4) if `suggested_capability` present, must match a name from discovery results. **Import**: `ScoredCapability` from `k1/fabric/types.py`. **Token accounting**: after LLM call, `stage_token_usage[SKETCH] = hub_response.metadata.usage.total_tokens`. |

Flows: ExpandService -> PipelineController -> ValidateService -> CommitService. `@dataclass(frozen=True)`. **Signature**: `ExpandedPlan(steps: List[PlanStep], dependencies: Dict[str, List[str]], tool_mappings: Dict[str, str], rationale: str)`. **PlanStep** imported from `k1/orchestrator/types.py` (14 frozen fields per §32). **Field population source matrix** (§7.4.2): 7 fields LLM-generated (id, capability, params, deps, prompt_template, output_schema, condition), 7 fields deterministically enriched by ExpandService from CapabilityContract metadata (tools_granted, is_optional, has_side_effects, compensation, timeout_ms, required_context, token_budget=V2 deferred). **dependencies format**: `{"s2": ["s1"], "s3": ["s1", "s2"]}` -- step_id -> list of dependency step_ids. Must form DAG (validated by ValidateService). |
| 1.2.5 | ValidationVerdict dataclass | DONE | k1/planner/types.py [F05] | **Internal type** (§8.3.3). Flows: ValidateService -> PipelineController -> CommitService (if approved). `@dataclass(frozen=True)`. **Signature**: `ValidationVerdict(status: str, issues: List[ValidationIssue], confidence: float, rationale: str)`. **status values**: `"approved"` (plan is valid, proceed to COMMIT), `"revise"` (send back to EXPAND with issues, max 1 revise loop), `"reject"` (PLAN FAILED). **ValidationIssue** (nested): `@dataclass(frozen=True) ValidationIssue(check_name: str, severity: str, step_id: Optional[str], detail: str)`. **severity values**: `"error"` (blocks commit), `"warning"` (logged, plan continues). **check_name values**: `"dag_cycle"`, `"capability_missing"`, `"param_type_mismatch"`, `"unsafe_capability"`, `"llm_arbiter_reject"`. **confidence**: float 0.0-1.0, from VALIDATE LLM arbiter. |
| 1.2.6 | StageContext dataclass | DONE | k1/planner/types.py [F05] | **Internal type** -- passed to every pipeline stage (§30.5.1 F05). `@dataclass(frozen=True)`. **Signature**: `StageContext(request_id: str, trace_id: str, timeout_remaining_ms: int, token_budget_remaining: int, cancel_check: Callable[[], bool])`. **Created by**: `PipelineController` at start of each stage, updated with remaining timeouts and budgets. **cancel_check**: callable that returns True if `request_id` is in `_cancel_set` -- enables cooperative cancellation between stages (§18.5.3). **trace_id propagation**: every outbound port call uses `ctx.trace_id` for FAB-09 compliance (§22.1). **Consumed by**: SketchService, ExpandService, ValidateService, CommitService -- each stage receives StageContext as second parameter alongside stage-specific input. **Tracing helper**: `create_stage_context(plan_request, config, cancel_check) -> StageContext` in `tracing.py` [F10]. |
| 1.2.7 | DeltaPayload types | DONE | k1/planner/types.py [F05] | **Internal type** for delta emissions (§22.4.1). `@dataclass(frozen=True)`. **Signature**: `DeltaPayload(agent_id: str, delta_type: str, section: str, data: Dict[str, Any], trace_id: str)`. **agent_id**: always `"planner"` (stamped by DeltaBusAdapter per §16.1.6). **section values**: `"pipeline"`, `"plan"`, `"tools"`. **11 delta types** (§28.3): `stage_started`, `stage_completed`, `plan_committed`, `plan_failed`, `plan_cancelled`, `tool_call_result`, `hil_clarification_sent`, `hil_approval_sent`, `micro_replan_started`, `micro_replan_completed`, `plan_end`. **Emission**: `IDeltaEmitPort.emit(DeltaPayload)` -- synchronous fire-and-forget, never raises, never blocks (§15.7). **Topic**: all deltas published to `k1.planner.delta.v1`. |
| 1.2.8 | PlanStep dataclass | DONE | k1/orchestrator/types.py (shared, already exists) | **Ownership**: Defined in `k1/orchestrator/types.py` per §32. **14 frozen fields** (§7.4.2): (1) `id: str` (e.g., "s1"), (2) `capability: str` (fully qualified name), (3) `params: Dict[str, Any]` (may contain `$s1.result.data.field` references), (4) `deps: List[str]` (dependency step IDs), (5) `prompt_template: Optional[str]` (for agent-type steps), (6) `tools_granted: Optional[List[str]]` (FAB-07 scoped tools), (7) `token_budget: int` (V1 REMOVED -- PLAN-07 deferred), (8) `output_schema: Optional[Dict]` (JSON Schema for output validation), (9) `condition: Optional[ConditionExpr]` (conditional edge), (10) `is_optional: bool` (default False), (11) `has_side_effects: bool` (default False, needs compensation on failure), (12) `compensation: Optional[str]` (compensation capability name), (13) `timeout_ms: Optional[int]` (per-step override), (14) `required_context: Optional[List[str]]` (SessionState sections). **Planner responsibility**: ExpandService populates all 14 fields; 7 from LLM, 7 from CapabilityContract metadata enrichment. **Action**: verify existing type, add re-export in `k1/planner/__init__.py`. |
| 1.2.9 | MicroReplanRequest dataclass | DONE | k1/orchestrator/types.py (shared, already exists) | **Ownership**: Defined in `k1/orchestrator/types.py` per §32. **Signature** (§4.5): `@dataclass(frozen=True) MicroReplanRequest(original_plan_id: str, completed_results: Dict[str, StepResult], remaining_steps: List[PlanStep], trace_id: str, request_id: str, discoveries: List[Discovery], failure_context: Optional[FailureContext])`. **Defaults**: request_id=uuid4(), discoveries=[], failure_context=None. **Validation**: original_plan_id non-empty; remaining_steps non-empty. **Contract with Planner** (§10): Planner receives completed_results (what succeeded), discoveries (new info), remaining_steps (what was planned), failure_context (what failed). Returns new CommittedPlan with replacement steps. **Delivery**: `IPlannerMailbox.micro_replan(MicroReplanRequest)` -- synchronous, 10s timeout, bypasses queue. **PLAN-12**: completed steps frozen, only remaining adjusted. Max 1 per DAG (ORCH-13). **Action**: verify existing type, add re-export. |

Terminal states: COMPLETED, FAILED, CANCELLED. Micro-replan states: MICRO_SKETCH, MICRO_EXPAND, MICRO_VALIDATE. **Defined in**: `plan_fsm.py` [F04]. **StagePhase**: `str, Enum` with `SKETCH, EXPAND, VALIDATE, COMMIT`. Used by PipelineController for budget injection lookup and token tracking. **ToolCallStatus**: `str, Enum` with `SUCCESS, TIMEOUT, ERROR, BUDGET_EXHAUSTED`. Used by ToolCallRouter for result classification. **Defined in**: `types.py` [F05]. **Gotcha**: PlanState lives in plan_fsm.py (not types.py) because PlanStateMachine uses it directly. StagePhase and ToolCallStatus live in types.py (Layer 0, no deps). |

Published on `k1.hil.clarification.v1` by HILCoordinator during SKETCH when ambiguity detected. **HILApprovalRequestPayload**: `@dataclass(frozen=True)`. **Signature** (§30.5.1 F06): `HILApprovalRequestPayload(request_id: str, summary: str, options: List[str], side_effects: List[str], safety_assessment: str)`. Published on `k1.hil.approval_request.v1` by HILCoordinator during VALIDATE for high-risk plans. **Note**: response payloads are received from Concierge via event subscription -- Planner does not define response types (Concierge owns those). **Defined in**: `events.py` [F06] alongside topic constants. |

Note: this type may already exist in `k1/model_hub/types.py` -- if so, import rather than redefine. **TokenUsageRecord** (internal): `@dataclass TokenUsageRecord(stage: str, prompt_tokens: int, completion_tokens: int, total_tokens: int)`. Tracked per-stage by PipelineController. **Budget table** (§13.3): SKETCH={max_tokens:2048, timeout_ms:8000, temp:0.7}, EXPAND={max_tokens:1024, timeout_ms:5000, temp:0.3}, VALIDATE={max_tokens:512, timeout_ms:3000, temp:0.2}. Total budget: ~3.5K tokens. **Micro-replan budgets**: Micro-SKETCH={max_tokens:1024, timeout_ms:5000}, Micro-EXPAND={max_tokens:512, timeout_ms:3000}, Micro-VALIDATE={max_tokens:256, timeout_ms:2000}. **Reporting**: total tokens reported in PLAN_END delta and structured log. |

| 1.2.14 | Error types: PlannerError taxonomy | DONE | k1/planner/types.py [F05] | **Exception hierarchy** for Planner-specific errors. **Base**: `class PlannerError(Exception): stage: str, request_id: str, trace_id: str`. **Subclasses**: (1) `SketchFailedError(PlannerError)` -- ERR_SKETCH_FAIL (§6.6), LLM failed after retry with simplified prompt. (2) `ExpandFailedError(PlannerError)` -- ERR_EXPAND_FAIL (§7.6), LLM failed but fallback to SKETCH output available. (3) `ValidateRejectedError(PlannerError)` -- ERR_VALIDATE_FAIL (§8.6), ValidationVerdict.status="reject". (4) `CommitFailedError(PlannerError)` -- ERR_COMMIT_FAIL (§9.6), plan assembly or WAL persist failure. (5) `BudgetExhaustedError(PlannerError)` -- PLAN-05 tool call limit or PLAN-11 token budget exceeded. (6) `MailboxFullError(PlannerError)` -- mailbox depth > 5 on enqueue attempt. (7) `PlanCancelledError(PlannerError)` -- cooperative cancellation detected between stages. (8) `HILTimeoutError(PlannerError)` -- HIL approval timeout (clarification timeout is not an error, best-effort continues). **Usage**: stage services raise specific subclasses; PipelineController catches and routes to FAILED FSM state + plan.failed.v1 event emission. |

> **Wiring**:
>
> - **File locations**: Planner-internal types in `k1/planner/types.py` [F05]. PlannerConfig in `k1/planner/config.py` [F07]. Event payloads in `k1/planner/events.py` [F06]. PlanState enum in `k1/planner/plan_fsm.py` [F04]. Shared types (PlanRequest, CommittedPlan, PlanStep, MicroReplanRequest, PlanAck) remain in `k1/orchestrator/types.py` -- Planner re-exports from `k1/planner/__init__.py` [F01].
> - **Dependency layering** (§30.6): types.py, events.py, config.py, plan_fsm.py are all Layer 0 (no internal deps). They import only from stdlib, `k1.orchestrator.types`, and `k1.fabric.types`. No file in Layer 0 imports from any service, port, or adapter module.
> - **Creation -> Consumption map**:
>   - **SketchResult** (1.2.3): Created by SketchService.execute() -> passed to PipelineController -> consumed by ExpandService.execute() as input.
>   - **ExpandedPlan** (1.2.4): Created by ExpandService.execute() -> passed to PipelineController -> consumed by ValidateService.execute() and CommitService.execute().
>   - **ValidationVerdict** (1.2.5): Created by ValidateService.execute() -> consumed by PipelineController (routes to COMMIT if approved, back to EXPAND if revise, to FAILED if reject).
>   - **StageContext** (1.2.6): Created by PipelineController at each stage entry -> consumed by all 4 stage services as second parameter.
>   - **DeltaPayload** (1.2.7): Created by any service needing delta emission -> consumed by IDeltaEmitPort.emit() -> DeltaBusAdapter stamps agent_id and publishes.
>   - **PlannerConfig** (1.2.13): Created by PlannerFactory from config dict at boot -> consumed by PlannerAgent constructor, distributed to services via factory wiring.
>   - **Error types** (1.2.14): Raised by stage services -> caught by PipelineController -> routes to FAILED FSM state + plan.failed.v1 event.
> - **Anti-hallucination**: All internal types are pure dataclasses with NO I/O, NO port references, NO service logic. Do NOT add async methods to dataclasses. Do NOT redefine shared types from k1/orchestrator/types.py -- import and re-export only.

> - **Schema locations**: `k1/contracts/schemas/orchestrator/` contains PlanRequest and MicroReplanRequest JSON Schemas (already exist per orchestrator plan 1.2.26/1.2.27).

### Epic 1.4: Port Definitions

**Goal**: Define all 7 hexagonal port Protocols as `typing.Protocol` classes with `@runtime_checkable`. Ports are pure interface definitions with no implementation logic.

> **Reference**: planner.md §15 (Port Architecture), §30.5.2 (Port Files F11-F18), §30.7 (Port-to-Service Assignment Matrix).
>
> - All ports are `typing.Protocol` classes with `@runtime_checkable` decorator.
> - Ports import ONLY from `k1/planner/types.py` and external type modules (k1.orchestrator.types, k1.fabric.types, k1.model_hub.types). NO circular imports.
> - Port files are Layer 1 in dependency graph (§30.6): import types only.
> - **Port-to-Service assignment** (§30.7): each service receives ONLY the ports it needs (principle of least privilege). PlannerAgent gets IMailboxPort+IEventPort. PipelineController gets IDeltaEmitPort+IEventPort. SketchService gets ILLMPort (+ 3 ports via ToolCallRouter). ExpandService gets ILLMPort (+ 2 ports via ToolCallRouter). ValidateService gets ILLMPort+IFabricRetrievalPort. CommitService gets IBridgePort+IDeltaEmitPort+IEventPort. ToolCallRouter gets IFabricRetrievalPort+IStateReadPort+IBridgePort. HILCoordinator gets ILLMPort+IEventPort.

| Issue | Title | Status | Deliverable | Details |
|-------|-------|--------|-------------|---------|
| 1.4.1 | IMailboxPort protocol | DONE | k1/planner/ports/mailbox_port.py [F12] | `@runtime_checkable class IMailboxPort(Protocol)`. **Methods** (§15.2, §4.2): `async def enqueue(self, request: PlanRequest) -> None` -- enqueue plan request for async processing, raises MailboxFullError on depth >= 5. `async def send_cancel(self, request_id: str) -> None` -- signal cancellation for in-flight plan, best-effort. `async def micro_replan(self, request: MicroReplanRequest) -> CommittedPlan` -- synchronous micro-replan, Planner responds inline (10s budget from caller side). **Consumer-driven contract**: structurally identical to `IPlannerMailbox` Protocol defined on Orchestrator side (§4.2). **Callers**: PlannerAgent (dequeue side), Orchestrator's PlannerAdapter (enqueue side). **Adapter**: MailboxAdapter [F28] (bounded asyncio.Queue, cancel_set, plan_lock). **Imports**: PlanRequest, MicroReplanRequest, CommittedPlan from k1.orchestrator.types. |
| 1.4.2 | ILLMPort protocol | DONE | k1/planner/ports/llm_port.py [F13] | `@runtime_checkable class ILLMPort(Protocol)`. **Methods** (§15.3): `async def execute(self, request: HubRequest) -> HubResponse` -- single LLM inference call routed to Model Hub. **HubRequest** contains: `capability` (CHAT or STRUCTURED), `payload` (ChatPayload or StructuredOutputPayload), `constraints` (RequestConstraints with max_tokens, timeout_ms, temperature), `trace_id`. **HubResponse** contains: `result` (CapabilityResult with content field), `metadata` (ResponseMetadata with usage.total_tokens, latency_ms, model_id). **5 LLM call types** (§13.1): LLM-1 SKETCH main, LLM-2 EXPAND main, LLM-3 VALIDATE arbiter, LLM-4a HIL clarification, LLM-4b HIL approval draft. Plus micro-replan variants LLM-M1/M2/M3. **Callers**: SketchService, ExpandService, ValidateService, HILCoordinator. NOT CommitService (PLAN-03). **Adapters**: TestLLMAdapter (V1), LLMGatewayAdapter (V2 production). **Imports**: HubRequest, HubResponse from k1.model_hub.types. |
| 1.4.3 | IFabricRetrievalPort protocol | DONE | k1/planner/ports/fabric_retrieval_port.py [F14] | `@runtime_checkable class IFabricRetrievalPort(Protocol)`. **Methods** (§15.4): `async def discover_capabilities(self, query: str, top_k: int = 5, trace_id: str = "") -> List[RetrievalResult]` -- discover capabilities matching query from Fabric Registry. `async def find_relevant_prompts(self, query: str, top_k: int = 3, trace_id: str = "") -> List[RetrievalResult]` -- find prompt templates for agent-type steps. **PLAN-06 enforcement**: this port has discovery methods ONLY -- no `invoke_capability`, no `execute`, no write methods. Read-only by design. **Callers**: ToolCallRouter (routes discover_capabilities in SKETCH/EXPAND, find_relevant_prompts in EXPAND), ValidateService (direct capability existence check via discover_capabilities). **Adapter**: FabricRetrievalAdapter [F30] wraps Fabric RetrievalEngine (in-process, <50ms). **Imports**: RetrievalResult from k1.fabric.types. |

Enforced at interface level -- the Protocol simply does not declare any write/mutate methods. This is the architectural guarantee that the Planner never writes SessionState. **StateSnapshot** (§11.3.2): frozen dataclass with `session_id: str`, `sections: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]`, `timestamp_ms: int`, `section_names: List[str]`. **Callers**: ToolCallRouter only (routes query_planning_context -> read_sections). Not called directly by any stage service. **Adapter**: SessionStateReadAdapter [F31] wraps ISessionStateReader (lock-free multi-reader, <10ms). **Imports**: SessionSnapshot from k1.fabric.ports.state_reader (or define locally if decoupling needed). |

If Delta Bus is unavailable, adapter logs warning and returns silently. Deltas are best-effort progress signals, not critical data. **DeltaPayload** (§22.4.1): `agent_id="planner"`, `delta_type` (11 types per §28.3), `section` ("pipeline"/"plan"/"tools"), `data` (Dict), `trace_id`. **Callers**: PipelineController (stage_started, stage_completed, plan_end, plan_failed, plan_cancelled), CommitService (plan_committed), ToolCallRouter (tool_call_result), HILCoordinator (hil_clarification_sent, hil_approval_sent). **Adapter**: DeltaBusAdapter [F33] pre-stamps agent_id and topic (k1.planner.delta.v1). **Imports**: DeltaPayload from k1.planner.types. |

>
> - **File locations**: All port files in `k1/planner/ports/` (F11-F18). 8 files total (7 port Protocols + 1 `__init__.py` re-export).
> - **Dependency layer**: Layer 1 (§30.6). Port files import ONLY from types modules (Layer 0). No port file imports from any service, adapter, or agent module.
> - **Port -> Adapter mapping** (§16): IMailboxPort -> MailboxAdapter (F28, production) / TestMailboxAdapter (test). ILLMPort -> LLMGatewayAdapter (F29, V2 production) / TestLLMAdapter (V1 + test). IFabricRetrievalPort -> FabricRetrievalAdapter (F30). IStateReadPort -> SessionStateReadAdapter (F31). IBridgePort -> BridgeAdapter (F32). IDeltaEmitPort -> DeltaBusAdapter (F33). IEventPort -> EventBusAdapter (F34). Each adapter `isinstance`-checks True against its Protocol at construction (via `@runtime_checkable`).
> - **Anti-hallucination**: Ports are pure Protocol classes with NO implementation logic, NO I/O, NO state, NO constructors beyond Protocol default. Do NOT add any method body beyond `...` (Ellipsis). Implementation is in adapters (Epic 6.1, M6).

### Epic 1.5: Event Schemas & Config

**Goal**: Define all event topic constants, event payload schemas, and PlannerConfig with environment overrides.

> **Reference**: planner.md §28 (Complete Event Catalog), §26 (Delta Bus & Event Bus), §30.5.1 F06 (events.py spec), §30.5.1 F07 (config.py spec), §12.1 (HIL Namespace Split), §21 (Performance Targets), §22 (Observability).
>
> - **Event topic constants** and **payload dataclasses** are defined in `k1/planner/events.py` [F06]. This is the single source of truth for all Planner event topic strings -- services import from here, never hardcode topic strings.
> - **PlannerConfig** is defined in `k1/planner/config.py` [F07]. Frozen dataclass loaded from K1 configuration at boot time. Immutable after construction.
> - **Published topics** (7 per §28): plan.ready.v1, plan.failed.v1, plan.cancelled.v1, micro_replan.ready.v1, delta.v1, hil.clarification.v1, hil.approval_request.v1.
> - **Subscribed topics** (4 per §28): plan.request.v1, plan.cancel.v1, hil.clarification_response.v1, hil.approval_response.v1.

| 1.5.1 | Event schema: plan.ready | DONE | k1/planner/events.py [F06] | **Topic constant**: `TOPIC_PLAN_READY = "k1.planner.plan.ready.v1"`. **Payload**: `CommittedPlan` directly (no wrapper needed per §30.5.1 F06). CommittedPlan carries: plan_id, request_id, intent, steps (List[PlanStep]), trace_id, dependencies, estimated_duration_ms, created_at. **Publisher**: CommitService via `IEventPort.publish(TOPIC_PLAN_READY, committed_plan)` at COMMIT stage completion (§9.2). **Consumer**: Orchestrator's `receive_plan()` handler via `IEventSubscriptionPort.subscribe("k1.planner.plan.ready.v1")`. Correlates via `request_id` to PendingPlanContext. **Timing**: emitted once per successful plan, after CommittedPlan assembly and WAL persist (fire-and-forget). |
| 1.5.2 | Event schema: plan.failed | DONE | k1/planner/events.py [F06] | **Topic constant**: `TOPIC_PLAN_FAILED = "k1.planner.plan.failed.v1"`. **Payload**: `@dataclass(frozen=True) PlanFailedPayload(request_id: str, stage: str, error_code: str, error_message: str, partial_state: Optional[Dict], tokens_used: int, duration_ms: int, trace_id: str)`. **stage values**: "SKETCH", "EXPAND", "VALIDATE", "COMMIT". **error_code values**: "ERR_SKETCH_FAIL", "ERR_EXPAND_FAIL", "ERR_VALIDATE_FAIL", "ERR_COMMIT_FAIL", "ERR_TIMEOUT", "ERR_BUDGET_EXCEEDED". **Publisher**: PipelineController when FSM transitions to FAILED state (§6.6, §7.6, §8.6, §9.6). **Consumer**: Orchestrator cleans up PendingPlanContext, may degrade future HIGH-tier to MEDIUM if CB_PLANNER trips (3 consecutive failures). |
| 1.5.3 | Event schema: plan.cancelled | DONE | k1/planner/events.py [F06] | **Topic constant**: `TOPIC_PLAN_CANCELLED = "k1.planner.plan.cancelled.v1"`. **Payload**: `@dataclass(frozen=True) PlanCancelledPayload(request_id: str, reason: str, stage: str, trace_id: str)`. **reason values**: "cancelled_before_start" (in cancel_set when dequeued per §23.2 step 2), "cancelled_between_stages" (cancel flag detected at STAGE_TRANSITION per §23.3 step 2), "shutdown" (mailbox drained during SHUTDOWN per §23.5). **Publisher**: PipelineController or PlannerAgent depending on cancel location. **Consumer**: Orchestrator's PendingPlanContext cleanup. **Timing**: emitted at most once per cancelled request. |
| 1.5.4 | Event schema: micro_replan.ready | DONE | k1/planner/events.py [F06] | **Topic constant**: `TOPIC_MICRO_REPLAN_READY = "k1.planner.micro_replan.ready.v1"`. **Payload**: `CommittedPlan` directly (same as plan.ready but distinct topic). **Publisher**: CommitService when micro-replan pipeline completes successfully. **Note**: micro_replan() is synchronous (returns CommittedPlan directly to caller via IPlannerMailbox.micro_replan), so this event is supplementary notification for other subscribers, not the primary delivery mechanism. **Consumer**: any interested observer (e.g., telemetry, audit). **Timing**: emitted once per successful micro-replan, alongside the synchronous return. |
| 1.5.5 | Event schema: hil.clarification | DONE | k1/planner/events.py [F06] | **Topic constant**: `TOPIC_HIL_CLARIFICATION = "k1.hil.clarification.v1"`. **Payload**: `@dataclass(frozen=True) HILClarificationPayload(request_id: str, question: str, context: Dict[str, Any], trace_id: str)`. **question**: LLM-generated clarification question based on detected ambiguity (§12.2). **context**: relevant planning context for Concierge to present alongside question. **Publisher**: HILCoordinator during SKETCH stage when SketchService detects ambiguity (multi-interpretation, missing context). **Consumer**: Concierge routes to user, collects response, publishes `k1.hil.clarification_response.v1`. **Timeout**: 60s default (`hil_clarification_timeout_ms`), proceed with best-effort on timeout (not a plan failure). **PLAN-10**: counts against 2-round budget. |

If no matching request (stale/duplicate), log warning and discard. **PlannerAgent subscribes**: `IEventPort.subscribe(TOPIC_HIL_CLARIFICATION_RESP, _on_hil_clarification)` at INIT. |
| 1.5.8 | Subscription schema: hil.approval_response | DONE | k1/planner/events.py [F06] | **Topic constant**: `TOPIC_HIL_APPROVAL_RESP = "k1.hil.approval_response.v1"`. **Subscribed by**: HILCoordinator at INIT phase (§23.1 step 3). **Expected payload** (defined by Concierge): contains `request_id` for correlation and `decision: str` ("approved", "rejected", "modified") plus optional `modifications: Dict`. **Handler**: `HILCoordinator.on_approval_response(event)` -- resolves the pending approval Future. If "approved" -> VALIDATE proceeds to COMMIT. If "rejected" -> PLAN FAILED. If "modified" -> VALIDATE loops back to EXPAND with modifications (counts as revise). **Correlation**: same request_id matching pattern as clarification. **PlannerAgent subscribes**: `IEventPort.subscribe(TOPIC_HIL_APPROVAL_RESP, _on_hil_approval)` at INIT. |

Unrecognized keys raise ValueError (fail-fast). **Environment integration**: K1 runtime loads config from environment variables or config file, passes override dict to `PlannerConfig.from_dict()` at PlannerFactory construction (§29.1 Phase 5). **Override examples**: `PLANNER_SKETCH_MAX_TOKENS=1500` overrides sketch_max_tokens, `PLANNER_PIPELINE_TIMEOUT_MS=30000` overrides pipeline_timeout_ms. **Validation** (`__post_init__`): all timeouts > 0; mailbox_max_depth in [1, 20]; temperatures in [0.0, 2.0]; max_tool_calls_per_plan in [1, 20]; max_hil_rounds in [0, 5]; total_token_budget >= sketch + expand + validate budgets. **Test support**: `PlannerConfig()` with all defaults is valid for testing -- no required overrides. **Cross-ref**: PlannerFactory.create_for_testing() uses default PlannerConfig (§30.5.1 F08). |

All services import topic constants from `k1.planner.events` -- NEVER hardcode topic strings elsewhere.

ToolCallRouter reads max_tool_calls_per_plan. HILCoordinator reads max_hil_rounds and timeout values. MailboxAdapter reads mailbox_max_depth.

## Milestone 2: Pipeline Infrastructure

> **Goal**: Build the core pipeline orchestration: Plan FSM, PipelineController, and PlannerAgent with mailbox loop.

### Epic 2.1: Plan FSM

**Goal**: Implement the Plan State Machine as a validated enum field with transition table, illegal-transition rejection, event emission hooks, and error-to-FAILED terminal paths. The FSM is NOT a separate runtime object -- it is an enum field (`_current_state: PlanState`) inside PipelineController, mutated at each stage boundary.

> **Reference**: planner.md §18 (Plan State Machine), §18.1 (State Catalog), §18.2-18.3 (State Diagrams), §18.4 (Transition Table -- 25 legal transitions), §18.5 (Terminal States -- COMPLETED/FAILED/CANCELLED with side effects), §18.6 (Lifecycle to FSM Mapping), §18.8 (Concurrency & Plan Lock), §18.9 (FSM Invariant Enforcement), §30.5.1 F04 (plan_fsm.py spec).
>
> - **State ownership**: PipelineController owns `_current_state: PlanState` field. PlanStateMachine is a thin helper class that encapsulates transition validation and callback invocation.
> - **Monotonicity rule**: no backward transitions except the explicit terminal -> IDLE reset (COMPLETED -> IDLE, FAILED -> IDLE, CANCELLED -> IDLE).
> - **Every state change** has 3 side effects: (1) structured log, (2) delta emission `k1.planner.delta.v1{type: "stage_transition", from, to}`, (3) monotonic enforcement.
> - **Micro-replan FSM** uses the same PlanStateMachine with MICRO_SKETCH/MICRO_EXPAND/MICRO_VALIDATE states.
> - **Dependency layer**: plan_fsm.py is Layer 0 (§30.6) -- imports only from stdlib and types.py. No circular deps.

| Issue | Title | Status | Deliverable | Details |
|-------|-------|--------|-------------|---------|
| 2.1.1 | PlanState enum (10 states) | DONE | k1/planner/plan_fsm.py [F04] | **Signature**: `class PlanState(str, Enum)` with 10 members. **Transient states** (active pipeline): `IDLE` (initial/resting, no plan in progress), `SKETCHING` (Stage 1 active -- discovery tools + LLM sketch), `EXPANDING` (Stage 2 active -- tool mapping + LLM expand), `VALIDATING` (Stage 3 active -- deterministic checks + LLM arbiter + optional HIL), `COMMITTING` (Stage 4 active -- plan assembly + WAL persist + event emit). **Micro-replan states** (abbreviated pipeline): `MICRO_SKETCH`, `MICRO_EXPAND`, `MICRO_VALIDATE` (COMMITTING reused for micro-replan Stage 4). **Terminal states** (plan outcome resolved): `COMPLETED` (plan committed and delivered), `FAILED` (unrecoverable after retry), `CANCELLED` (cancelled by Orchestrator via send_cancel). **Helper properties**: `@property is_terminal -> bool` (COMPLETED, FAILED, CANCELLED), `@property is_micro -> bool` (MICRO_SKETCH, MICRO_EXPAND, MICRO_VALIDATE), `@property is_active -> bool` (not IDLE and not terminal). **Gotcha**: PlanState lives in plan_fsm.py (not types.py) because PlanStateMachine uses it directly. Already defined as enum in 1.2.10 -- this issue implements the file with full helpers. **Import**: `from enum import Enum`. |
| 2.1.2 | FSM transition table | DONE | k1/planner/plan_fsm.py [F04] | **Signature**: `TRANSITION_TABLE: Dict[PlanState, Set[PlanState]]` -- module-level constant mapping source state to set of legal target states. **25 legal transitions** (§18.4): **Full pipeline** (7): IDLE->SKETCHING, SKETCHING->EXPANDING, EXPANDING->VALIDATING, VALIDATING->COMMITTING, VALIDATING->EXPANDING (revise, max 1), COMMITTING->COMPLETED, COMPLETED->IDLE. **Micro-replan** (5): IDLE->MICRO_SKETCH, MICRO_SKETCH->MICRO_EXPAND, MICRO_EXPAND->MICRO_VALIDATE, MICRO_VALIDATE->COMMITTING, (COMMITTING->COMPLETED reused). **Error paths** (5): SKETCHING->FAILED, EXPANDING->FAILED, VALIDATING->FAILED (3 paths: reject after retry, HIL reject, arbiter reject), COMMITTING->CANCELLED (before persist). **Cancel paths** (4): SKETCHING->CANCELLED, EXPANDING->CANCELLED, VALIDATING->CANCELLED, COMMITTING->CANCELLED. **Terminal reset** (3): COMPLETED->IDLE, FAILED->IDLE, CANCELLED->IDLE. **Micro-replan errors** (3): MICRO_SKETCH->FAILED, MICRO_EXPAND->FAILED, MICRO_VALIDATE->FAILED. **Validation**: any transition NOT in this table is illegal (`IllegalStateTransitionError`). **Gotcha**: VALIDATING->EXPANDING is the only "backward" transition (revise loop) -- it is explicitly legal but guarded by `revise_count < 1` in PipelineController. |
| 2.1.3 | FSM transition validation (illegal transition rejection) | DONE | k1/planner/plan_fsm.py [F04] | **Signature**: `class PlanStateMachine` with `__init__(self, on_transition: Optional[Callable[[PlanState, PlanState, str], None]] = None)`. **Internal state**: `_current_state: PlanState = PlanState.IDLE`. **Methods**: `transition(self, new_state: PlanState, trigger: str = "") -> PlanState` -- validates that `(self._current_state, new_state)` is in TRANSITION_TABLE, updates `_current_state`, calls `on_transition` callback, returns new state. Raises `IllegalStateTransitionError(from_state, to_state, trigger)` if not in table. `@property current_state -> PlanState`. `reset(self) -> None` -- only legal from terminal states, transitions to IDLE. **IllegalStateTransitionError**: `class IllegalStateTransitionError(PlannerError)` with `from_state: PlanState`, `to_state: PlanState`, `trigger: str`. **PLAN-12 relationship**: if an illegal transition is attempted, the raised exception propagates to PipelineController's try/except wrapper which catches it and routes to FAILED (via `force_failed()`). **Monotonicity**: forward-only within a pipeline run; the only backward transition is the explicit VALIDATING->EXPANDING revise (legal in table). Terminal->IDLE is the only reset path. **Gotcha**: `transition()` MUST call the callback AFTER updating `_current_state` (observer sees new state). |
| 2.1.4 | FSM event emission on transitions | DONE | k1/planner/plan_fsm.py [F04] | **Mechanism**: PlanStateMachine constructor accepts an optional `on_transition: Callable[[PlanState, PlanState, str], None]` callback. **Callback contract**: invoked after every successful `transition()` call with `(from_state, to_state, trigger)`. PipelineController provides the callback implementation that: (1) emits `k1.planner.delta.v1{type: "stage_transition", from: from_state.value, to: to_state.value}` via IDeltaEmitPort, (2) writes structured log `plan.state_transition{request_id, from, to, trigger, trace_id}`, (3) updates internal metrics. **Delta payload** (§22.4.1): `DeltaPayload(agent_id="planner", delta_type="stage_transition", section="pipeline", data={"from": from_state.value, "to": to_state.value, "trigger": trigger, "timestamp_ms": now_ms()}, trace_id=current_trace_id)`. **No callback for terminal->IDLE**: the reset path (COMPLETED/FAILED/CANCELLED -> IDLE) emits a separate `plan_end` delta from PipelineController's PLAN_END handler, not from the FSM callback. **Gotcha**: the callback MUST NOT raise -- PipelineController wraps it in try/except to prevent delta emission failures from blocking pipeline execution. If emit fails, log warning and continue. |
| 2.1.5 | FSM error-to-FAILED terminal transitions | DONE | k1/planner/plan_fsm.py [F04] | **Signature**: `force_failed(self, trigger: str = "") -> PlanState` -- transitions any active state (SKETCHING, EXPANDING, VALIDATING, COMMITTING, MICRO_SKETCH, MICRO_EXPAND, MICRO_VALIDATE) to FAILED. Does NOT validate against TRANSITION_TABLE (override). Raises `IllegalStateTransitionError` if called from IDLE, COMPLETED, FAILED, or CANCELLED (cannot fail a non-active plan). **5 entry paths to FAILED** (§18.5.2): (1) SKETCHING -- ERR_SKETCH_FAIL (LLM fail after retry, error_code=SKETCH_EXHAUSTED), (2) EXPANDING -- ERR_EXPAND_FAIL (LLM + fallback fail, error_code=EXPAND_EXHAUSTED), (3) VALIDATING -- verdict "reject" after retry EXPAND (error_code=VALIDATE_REJECTED), (4) VALIDATING -- HIL "reject" response (error_code=HIL_REJECTED), (5) any active state -- uncaught exception wrapped by PLAN-12 (error_code=INTERNAL_ERROR). **Side effects on FAILED entry** (managed by PipelineController, not FSM): emit `k1.planner.plan.failed.v1{request_id, stage, error_code, error_message, partial_state, tokens_used, duration_ms, trace_id}`, emit delta `{type: "plan_failed", stage, error_code}`, release plan lock, clear counters, transition to IDLE. **Micro-replan variant**: FAILED does NOT emit plan.failed.v1 (micro-replan is best-effort), PipelineController returns None to caller. **`force_cancelled(self, trigger: str = "") -> PlanState`**: similar method for cancel, transitions active states to CANCELLED. |

> **Wiring**:
>
> - **File location**: `k1/planner/plan_fsm.py` [F04]. Single file contains PlanState enum, TRANSITION_TABLE constant, PlanStateMachine class, and IllegalStateTransitionError.
> - **Dependency layer**: Layer 0 (§30.6). Imports only from stdlib (`enum`, `typing`) and `k1.planner.types` (PlannerError base class). No service, port, or adapter imports.
> - **Consumers**: PipelineController (2.2.1) creates a PlanStateMachine instance and provides the `on_transition` callback for delta emission. PlannerAgent (2.3.1) reads `_pipeline._fsm.current_state` for lifecycle decisions.
> - **Testing**: ~15 tests (§17.2 test categories "State machine transitions"): legal transitions succeed, illegal transitions raise, terminal reset works, callback invoked on each transition, force_failed from each active state, force_cancelled from each active state, monotonicity enforcement.
> - **Anti-hallucination**: PlanStateMachine is a pure state container with validation logic. It does NOT do I/O, does NOT hold port references, does NOT emit deltas directly. Delta emission is the CALLER's responsibility via the callback mechanism.

### Epic 2.2: PipelineController

**Goal**: Implement the top-level pipeline orchestrator that sequences the 4 stages (SKETCH -> EXPAND -> VALIDATE -> COMMIT), injects per-stage budgets, checks cancel flags between stages, routes micro-replan through the abbreviated pipeline, enforces the 45s global timeout, and constructs StageContext per stage.

> **Reference**: planner.md §17.2 (PipelineController -- ~80 tests), §5.2 (26-Step Pipeline Flow), §13.3 (Budget Injection), §10 (Micro-Replan), §18 (Plan FSM), §23.3 (STAGE_TRANSITION lifecycle), §24.2 (Cancellation), §30.5.1 F03 (pipeline_controller.py spec).
>
> - **Constructor** (§17.2): `PipelineController(sketch: SketchService, expand: ExpandService, validate: ValidateService, commit: CommitService, delta_port: IDeltaEmitPort, event_port: IEventPort)`.
> - **Ports used** (direct): IDeltaEmitPort, IEventPort. Stage services have their own port references.
> - **Invariants enforced**: PLAN-03 (CommitService last, no LLM), PLAN-04 (45s timeout check at each transition), PLAN-11 (per-stage budget injection), PLAN-12 (try/except wraps execute(), uncaught -> FAILED).
> - **Test categories** (~80 tests per §17.2): happy-path sequencing (~15), budget injection correctness (~15), cancel between stages (~10), error propagation (~15), state machine transitions (~15), delta emission (~10).

Called at PLAN_START (§23.2 step 4). **Dependency layer**: Layer 2 (§30.6) -- imports Layer 1 ports and Layer 0 types. |
| 2.2.2 | Stage orchestration loop (SKETCH -> EXPAND -> VALIDATE -> COMMIT) | DONE | k1/planner/pipeline_controller.py [F03] | **Signature**: `async def execute(self, request: PlanRequest, cancel_check: Callable[[], bool]) -> CommittedPlan`. **Flow** (§5.2 steps 4-26): (1) `reset()` -- clear counters, set FSM to IDLE. (2) FSM IDLE->SKETCHING, emit delta(SKETCH started). (3) `sketch_result = await _sketch.execute(request, stage_ctx)`. (4) Check cancel flag (`cancel_check()`), check timeout. (5) FSM SKETCHING->EXPANDING, emit delta(SKETCH completed, tokens). (6) `expanded_plan = await _expand.execute(sketch_result, stage_ctx)`. (7) Check cancel, check timeout. (8) FSM EXPANDING->VALIDATING, emit delta(EXPAND completed, tokens). (9) `verdict = await _validate.execute(expanded_plan, stage_ctx)`. (10) **Verdict routing**: if "approved" -> proceed to COMMIT. If "revise" and `_revise_count < 1` -> increment `_revise_count`, FSM VALIDATING->EXPANDING, loop back to step 6 with `verdict.suggested_fixes` injected. If "reject" after retry -> FSM VALIDATING->FAILED. (11) FSM VALIDATING->COMMITTING. (12) `committed = await _commit.execute(expanded_plan, verdict, stage_ctx)`. (13) FSM COMMITTING->COMPLETED. (14) Return CommittedPlan. **Outer wrapper**: entire body in `try/except Exception` -- any uncaught exception triggers `_fsm.force_failed()`, emits plan.failed.v1, re-raises (PLAN-12). **Gotcha**: stage services are called sequentially (not concurrently). Each stage may internally use asyncio.gather for concurrent tool calls, but stages themselves are serial. |
| 2.2.3 | Budget injection per stage | DONE | k1/planner/pipeline_controller.py [F03] | **Budget table** (§13.3, §23.3 step 6): SKETCH=`{max_tokens: 2048, timeout_ms: 8000, temperature: 0.7}`, EXPAND=`{max_tokens: 1024, timeout_ms: 5000, temperature: 0.3}`, VALIDATE=`{max_tokens: 512, timeout_ms: 3000, temperature: 0.2}`, COMMIT=no LLM (PLAN-03, budget=None). **Signature**: `_get_stage_budget(self, stage: StagePhase) -> RequestConstraints` -- returns constraints from PlannerConfig for the given stage. **Injection point**: PipelineController stamps the budget into StageContext before calling each stage service. The stage service reads `ctx.token_budget` and passes it to `HubRequest.constraints`. **Token tracking**: `_record_llm_call(self, stage: StagePhase, response: HubResponse) -> None` -- updates `_stage_token_usage[stage] += response.metadata.usage.total_tokens`, `_total_plan_tokens += ...`, `_stage_latency[stage] += response.metadata.latency_ms`. **Delta emission**: token usage included in stage_completed delta payload. **Micro-replan budgets** (§10.3.6): MICRO_SKETCH=`{max_tokens: 1024, timeout_ms: 5000}`, MICRO_EXPAND=`{max_tokens: 512, timeout_ms: 3000}`, MICRO_VALIDATE=`{max_tokens: 256, timeout_ms: 2000}`. Separate budget table loaded from PlannerConfig. **PLAN-11 enforcement**: every LLM call MUST have budget -- no unbounded calls. Stage services MUST use the injected budget, not hardcoded values. |
| 2.2.4 | Cancel-between-stages protocol | DONE | k1/planner/pipeline_controller.py [F03] | **Cancel check method**: `_check_cancel(self, cancel_check: Callable[[], bool], stage: str) -> None`. If `cancel_check()` returns True: FSM -> CANCELLED via `_fsm.force_cancelled(trigger=f"cancelled_between_{stage}")`, emit `plan.cancelled.v1{request_id, reason: "cancelled_between_stages", stage, trace_id}`, raise `PlanCancelledError`. **5 cancel checkpoints** (§24.2.2): (1) pre-execution at mailbox dequeue (PlannerAgent, before lock), (2) between SKETCH and EXPAND, (3) between EXPAND and VALIDATE, (4) between VALIDATE and COMMIT, (5) before WAL persist in CommitService. **Cancel timing** (§24.2.1): cooperative, NOT preemptive. If an LLM call is in flight, cancel is not checked until the LLM returns (or times out). The LLM call completes, PipelineController detects cancel at the next checkpoint. **Cancel semantics** (§18.5.3): `cancel_check` is a closure over `_cancel_set: Set[str]` -- returns True if `current_request.request_id in _cancel_set`. **Side effects**: emit `k1.planner.plan.cancelled.v1` event, emit delta `{type: "plan_cancelled", request_id, reason}`, discard partial results. PipelineController does NOT release the plan lock (that is PlannerAgent's responsibility in PLAN_END). **Edge case**: cancel during COMMIT before WAL persist -> CANCELLED (plan never written). After WAL persist -> too late, plan is COMPLETED. |
| 2.2.5 | Micro-replan routing | DONE | k1/planner/pipeline_controller.py [F03] | **Signature**: `async def micro_replan(self, request: MicroReplanRequest, cancel_check: Callable[[], bool]) -> Optional[CommittedPlan]`. **Abbreviated pipeline** (§10.3): MICRO_SKETCH -> MICRO_EXPAND -> MICRO_VALIDATE -> COMMIT (reuses normal COMMITTING state). Skips full EXPAND (3 stages instead of 4). **Flow**: (1) reset(), FSM IDLE->MICRO_SKETCH. (2) `micro_sketch_result = await _sketch.micro_execute(request)` -- uses MicroReplanRequest.remaining_steps, completed_results, discoveries, failure_context. (3) Cancel check + FSM MICRO_SKETCH->MICRO_EXPAND. (4) `micro_expanded = await _expand.micro_execute(micro_sketch_result, request.completed_results)`. (5) Cancel check + FSM MICRO_EXPAND->MICRO_VALIDATE. (6) `verdict = await _validate.micro_execute(micro_expanded)`. (7) **Verdict routing**: "approved" or "revise" (treated as approved, no revise loop in micro) -> proceed. "reject" -> return None. (8) FSM MICRO_VALIDATE->COMMITTING, commit, COMPLETED. Return CommittedPlan with replacement steps only. **Differences from full pipeline**: no HIL interaction (§10.5.4), no revise loop (§10.6.2), verdict "revise" = "approved", on failure return None (no plan.failed event), separate token budget table (§10.3.6), separate counters. **Timing**: 10s total budget (caller-enforced by PlannerAdapter.asyncio.wait_for), per-stage: MICRO_SKETCH ~5s, MICRO_EXPAND ~3s, MICRO_VALIDATE ~2s, COMMIT <0.1s. **PLAN-12 compliance**: completed_results from MicroReplanRequest are FROZEN -- micro-replan only produces replacement steps for remaining_steps. |
| 2.2.6 | Pipeline timeout enforcement (45s, PLAN-04) | DONE | k1/planner/pipeline_controller.py [F03] | **Mechanism**: PipelineController checks elapsed time at each stage transition (§18.9). **Signature**: `_check_timeout(self) -> None`. Computes `elapsed_ms = (now() - _plan_start_time) * 1000`. If `elapsed_ms > config.pipeline_timeout_ms` (default 45000): FSM -> FAILED via `force_failed(trigger="pipeline_timeout")`, emit `plan.failed.v1{request_id, stage: current_stage, error_code: "PIPELINE_TIMEOUT", error_message: f"Pipeline exceeded {config.pipeline_timeout_ms}ms", tokens_used: _total_plan_tokens, duration_ms: elapsed_ms}`, raise `PlannerError("pipeline_timeout")`. **Called at**: every `_check_cancel()` site (5 checkpoints), making it 5 timeout checks per full pipeline. **PLAN-04 distinction**: PLAN-04 is enforced by Orchestrator's CB_PLANNER (45s external timeout). This internal check is defense-in-depth: PipelineController self-terminates before CB_PLANNER trips, producing a clean FAILED event instead of an abrupt timeout. **Micro-replan timeout**: 10s total, but enforced by CALLER (PlannerAdapter.asyncio.wait_for), not by PipelineController internally. PipelineController uses `config.micro_replan_timeout_ms` for its internal check. **Gotcha**: timer starts at `reset()` (PLAN_START step 4), not at mailbox dequeue. Mailbox wait time is excluded from the 45s budget. |
| 2.2.7 | StageContext construction & propagation | DONE | k1/planner/pipeline_controller.py [F03] | **Signature**: `_create_stage_context(self, stage: StagePhase) -> StageContext`. **StageContext** (from 1.2.6): `StageContext(request_id, trace_id, timeout_remaining_ms, token_budget_remaining, cancel_check)`. **Per-stage construction**: `timeout_remaining_ms = config.pipeline_timeout_ms - elapsed_ms` (shrinks as pipeline progresses), `token_budget_remaining = config.total_token_budget - _total_plan_tokens` (shrinks as tokens consumed), `cancel_check = lambda: self._current_request.request_id in cancel_set` (closure over cancel set). `request_id` and `trace_id` echoed from `_current_request`. **Updated at each stage boundary**: new StageContext constructed before each stage call, reflecting consumed time and tokens. **trace_id propagation** (FAB-09): every outbound port call uses `ctx.trace_id`. Stage services pass `ctx.trace_id` to `HubRequest.trace_id`, `discover_capabilities(trace_id=ctx.trace_id)`, `recall(trace_id=ctx.trace_id)`, delta emissions, structured logs. **Consumed by**: all 4 stage services receive StageContext as second parameter alongside stage-specific input (e.g., `sketch.execute(request, ctx)`, `expand.execute(sketch_result, ctx)`). **Helper**: static method or module function `create_stage_context(request, config, elapsed_ms, tokens_used, cancel_check) -> StageContext` in `tracing.py` [F10] for reuse. |

> **Wiring**:
>
> - **File location**: `k1/planner/pipeline_controller.py` [F03]. Single file contains PipelineController class.
> - **Dependency layer**: Layer 2 (§30.6). Imports from Layer 1 (ports: IDeltaEmitPort, IEventPort), Layer 0 (types: PlanRequest, CommittedPlan, StageContext, PlannerConfig, PlanState, StagePhase, all error types), and Layer 2 (services: SketchService, ExpandService, ValidateService, CommitService -- injected).
> - **FSM ownership**: PipelineController creates `PlanStateMachine(on_transition=self._on_fsm_transition)` in constructor. The FSM instance is private; external code reads state via `pipeline.current_state` property.
> - **Service delegation**: PipelineController calls `service.execute(input, ctx)` for each stage. It does NOT reach into service internals. Stage output flows: SketchResult -> ExpandedPlan -> ValidationVerdict -> CommittedPlan.
> - **Testing**: ~80 tests covering: happy-path 4-stage sequencing, micro-replan 3-stage, budget injection correctness per stage, cancel at each inter-stage boundary, error propagation from each stage, timeout enforcement, revise loop, FSM transition correctness, delta emission per transition.
> - **Anti-hallucination**: PipelineController orchestrates but does NOT perform LLM calls, tool discovery, or HIL interaction directly. Those are service responsibilities. PipelineController's direct I/O is limited to delta emissions (IDeltaEmitPort) and lifecycle event publishing (IEventPort).
